Qatar & Middle East — Daily·Weekly Media Monitor

Twice a day (07:00 and 15:30 Qatar time), major coverage from Qatari, Iranian, other major countries' and Korean outlets is compiled into AI issue summaries with source links. A list of the latest reports from relevant institutions is also available for reference lower on the page, and every Sunday a weekly report recapping the past week is published. Issue 1

⚓ Iran Announces Plan to Declare 'Restricted/Prohibited Zone' Near Strait of Hormuz Amid Escalating US-Iran Naval Clashes

Key summary
  • Mohsen Rezaei, Secretary of Iran's Supreme National Security Council, announced that Iran will soon declare a restricted/prohibited zone near the Strait of Hormuz, and will simultaneously unveil a new alternative route agreed upon with Oman. In response to the US attacking and destroying three Iranian oil tankers, Iranian Parliament Speaker Mohammad Bagher Ghalibaf warned of a stronger response should further attacks occur.

Issue 2

🛢️ International Oil Prices Surge, Asian Stock Markets Fluctuate — Supply Disruption Concerns Amid US-Iran Escalation

Key summary
  • As the US and Iran continue to strike each other's vessels in the Strait of Hormuz, international oil prices (Brent) have risen for consecutive days, with WSJ, Reuters, CNBC and others all reporting the price surge driven by supply disruption concerns.
  • Asian stock markets showed mixed signals as the KOSPI rose more than 3% on a semiconductor rally, while oil prices simultaneously spiked following news of tanker strikes near Hormuz, blending energy-driven risk with equity market strength.

Issue 3

💥 Israel Continues Airstrikes on Lebanon and Gaza — Evacuation Orders Targeting Hezbollah and Destruction of Nabatieh Hospital

Key summary
  • The Arab League issued a statement condemning the attack on Nabatieh in southern Lebanon.
  • In Gaza, an 8-year-old girl and her father were killed in a vehicle strike on the first day of the new school year.
  • Eight Arab countries, including Qatar, issued a joint statement condemning Israel's remarks on the forced displacement of Palestinians. The Muslim World League separately condemned Israel's remarks on the forced displacement of Palestinians as well.

Issue 4

🇶🇦 Qatar Hosts AIIB Annual Meeting, Discusses Infrastructure and Energy Cooperation with Egypt and the Region

Key summary
  • Qatar hosted the Asian Infrastructure Investment Bank (AIIB) Annual Meeting in Doha, where Egypt sought to expand cooperation with AIIB in the fields of energy, transportation, ports, and logistics.

This page automatically collects and summarizes public news coverage and material from relevant institutions across Qatar, Iran, other major countries and Korea. News is gathered by querying Google News RSS feeds, Naver News search, and outlet RSS (Al Jazeera, BBC, etc.) with keywords such as Qatar, the Middle East and energy; in-depth reports are collected directly from the publication boards and RSS of domestic and international research institutes, international organizations and consultancies (supplemented by news search targeting those publishers), keeping only analyses and reports on the Middle East situation, the Iran war and their industrial, supply-chain and energy repercussions. The report list accumulates items published since the war's outbreak (2026-02-28), split into domestic and overseas and ordered by publication date (newest first). Collection and summarization rely on each outlet's publicly available headline and snippet; for subscription or paywalled outlets, the full article can be opened through the provided source link only after the reader signs in or subscribes to that outlet directly. The collected articles are automatically clustered and summarized by issue, and translated into English and Arabic, by Anthropic Claude (Sonnet 5) AI. The whole process runs and publishes automatically twice a day (07:00 and 15:30 Qatar time) via GitHub Actions. The Qatar government activity section separately curates only official statements, announcements and actions on the war and regional situation by Qatar government and state entities — the Emir, the Prime Minister, the ministries of Foreign Affairs, Defense and Interior, the Government Communications Office (GCO), QatarEnergy and the state news agency (QNA). Owing to automated collection some articles or reports may be missed, so please re-verify important issues against the original sources and further searches.
- Embassy of the Republic of Korea in Qatar, Commercial Section · KOTRA Doha
